**Reviewer checklist**: scan the "Passed" table above for any demo/soundtrack/tool-like titles that slipped through the suffix filter (known gap: mid-title occurrences like "X - Soundtrack and Bonus Content" won't be caught). To reject an entry, remove it from `candidates.json` in this PR before merging. To permanently block an App ID from ever being suggested again, add it to `discovery-data/refs/rejected_appids.txt` in the same commit. To rescue a wrongly-excluded title from the Pending or pre-filter tables (e.g. a legitimate game caught by the suffix heuristic), run `python updater/main.py --force-include <appid>` before the next scheduled run; it will be fetched fresh and re-evaluated on the next discovery run.
